# Catchline — Codex communications

This is the canonical shared log for Windows Codex, the WSL CLI lead and their
explicitly authorized subagents. Current Mo assignments and the latest task status
take precedence over historical role splits below. Read latest entries before work.

**Stable identities:** Codex 1 = Windows Codex; Codex 2 = this existing WSL CLI.
Historical Windows orchestrator / WSL lead or artist names map to these identities.
Both platform-global `.codex/AGENTS.md` files contain Mo's automatic diary prompt.

- Windows: `C:\Users\momoy\Projects\Catchline\comms.md`
- WSL: `/mnt/c/Users/momoy/Projects/Catchline/comms.md`
- Main game: `Catchline`, branch `proto/deck-sim`.
- Art worktree: `Catchline-art-style`, branch `art/style-pass`.

## Mo's ownership split — 2026-10-15

**Historical assignment:** Mo subsequently authorized WSL gameplay/UI/economy
and delegated fish art to GPT-6.1 Sol on 2026-10-16. This old split does not override
those assignments. Current reservations/status are recorded in latest task entries.

| Agent | Assignment |
| --- | --- |
| Windows Codex | Orchestrator: game design, mechanics, shops and menus, movement, gameplay, Unity scenes/code, runtime visual integration and verification. |
| WSL Codex (this conversation) | 3D models only: Blender sources, FBX exports, geometry, asset materials within the approved palette, rigs/model animations where required, renders, validation and model handoffs. |

WSL Codex has relinquished gameplay ownership to Windows Codex. It will not edit game scripts, shop/menu UI, movement, scenes or runtime systems under this assignment. Windows Codex owns importing/placing the delivered models and configuring runtime materials, lighting and water effects. Coordinate changes to shared model names, pivots, rig contracts and dimensions here before depending on them.

## Communication protocol — every prompt

1. Read this file before work.
2. Append a **START** entry before editing: agent, prompt/task ID, intent, paths/model names, expected delivery and integration needs.
3. Append a **DONE** entry when finished: actual changes, commit, validation, preview/report paths, unfinished work and what the other Codex needs to do. Use **PARTIAL/BLOCKED** honestly when appropriate.
4. Preserve the other agent's entries. Append new entries rather than rewriting their history. Keep this main-project file authoritative even while working in the art worktree.
5. Windows Codex appends an integration acknowledgement with the accepted art commit and native Unity result. An asset delivery is not a claim of gameplay integration.
6. Each START also states exact write reservations, planned checks, collaborators
   and subagent scopes. Record changed scope/delegation in an UPDATE immediately.
7. Each closing entry lists actual files/artifacts/checks, native checks still
   pending, commit or no commit, lead/subagent state and explicit released/held
   reservations. Describe what another agent may pick up and what is actively owned.
8. When Mo says stop or asks for pickup, append STOPPED/PARTIAL with a usable
   checkpoint and release stopped reservations. Do not auto-resume historical work.
   The latest status for each task supersedes its START; DONE/idle does not hold files.
9. The lead owns logging delegated work here. Subagents append their own START/DONE
   when instructed, and the lead consolidates their status/results. No separate
   comm file should compete with this canonical log.
10. Keep ordered step IDs and CHECKPOINT during a turn: last VERIFIED step/evidence,
    implemented-but-UNVERIFIED work, IN PROGRESS step and exact next action. Mark
    each step before starting and checkpoint after meaningful safe boundaries.
11. Pickup starts at the first unfinished step, checking its actual code/artifacts.
    If Codex 1 verified D and stopped during E, Codex 2 checks/finishes E then F.
    Do not redo A–D unless dependencies/evidence require it. Missing completion
    does not mean the interrupted agent wrote nothing. Log scope-specific takeover.

### CHECKPOINT — Codex 1 — WEIGHT019 B VERIFIED; C IN PROGRESS
Arithmetic from current compact-net source: expected3.8fish; Harbour-mouth filtered60/40herring/mackerel at mean size parameter.3 =>1.03512kg and15.68184coins/haul before market/freshness/rounding. Worker80%safe =>12.54547 expected gross vs16 expected replacement expense (80*.2), demonstrating current provisional net imbalance. Plain weight conversion to10kg is not pacing-neutral. Herring17/kg exceeds cod9/conger7, so desired large-size premium requires an explicit capped species-relative size multiplier, not unit-label replacement.
Proposed discussion example (not approved tuning): 10kg schoolfish at10/kg=100;10kg specimens at15/kg=150. Deep net5kg*14/kg*3uses=210gross,110after100purchase before all voyage expenses; with20%destruction ending remaining uses, expected successful hauls .8+.64+.512=1.952 =>136.64gross/36.64after purchase, substantially less than simply applying.8toall3hauls. Conditions/local pool and shared hold must constrain projected returns.
Recommended overflow semantics: accept one whole fish if exact stored kg is below limit, then latch full; atomic single-fish inserts across concurrent workers and net unloading. Whole net batch does not count as one final fish; retain remainder explicitly. Fullness/journal/market/contracts remain distinct units where justified. Proposed staged rollout: weight storage/save/HUD/upgrades + pacing; capped size pricing/weight-based market; shallow/deep net budgets/wear; then visible conditions/journal/rank. No implementation performed.

### CHECKPOINT / PARTIAL — CREW076 package for Mo review

ACCEPTED DIRECTION: Back-to-Back Netter can reset own net while prior landed catch remains, with finite two-haul basket. No deletion/autostow/repair/net-use exemption. Mo wants extra production to increase tool strain, not lower it. Do not assume all rarity cards receive one blanket wear multiplier; define operating burdens per signature in later roster audit.
PROPOSED FULL PACKAGE, NOT FINAL: one automatic active+two team passives. Signature redeploy before own prior catch clears, maximumtwo landed haul slots; pause redeploy at fullbasket, resume when a haul slot clears, still needs operator for set/haul, ordinary fullhold stops remain. Working-style cost: own hauled cycles consume50%more operating condition than ordinary hauling, no multiplier on unrelated rigs or passive anchored time; more cycles add wear too. Passive1 Clear Deck: any team's net haul landed into an empty basket has25%extra catch, also normal extra handling/weight-linked strain, no hold enlargement. Passive2 Fresh to Market: any net-caught fish processed within60seconds of landing gains20%extra sale value, ordinary quality/freshness still apply; unprocessed/later processed fish simply get normal values. This creates volume-versus-timely-processing tension with bounded surge basket rather than removing pressure. All values tunable, timing/batch attribution/condition-workload formulas need future concrete implementation. Signature's own wear surcharge is a trait/cost, not an extra counted skillslot.
REQUIRED FOUNDATION PROPOSED: carrying effort must scale with kg through bounded carry loads and/or kg-linked stowing time. Current whole-basket fixed-action shortcut would otherwise undermine deckhand pressure. No such baseline redesign authorized for implementation now. Future management screen should expose incomingkg/min, actual transportkg/min, basket occupancy and condition/service rate, plus eligible processing timer, without manual ability buttons.

PROPOSED ROD: Twin-Hook Specialist,1active2passives. Own assigned rod can work two hooks from one cast, each separate normal bite timer, fisher strikes/reels each fish sequentially (no concurrent extra rod, no replacing another fisher); waiting hook can progress while first reeled, with finite2fish pending queue and ordinary failure/landing/carry rules. New actor/timer rule requires actual implementation, no promise current rod supports it. Each landed fish creates normal downstream work and wear; extra strain surcharge tunable. Team rod wait20%less duration, cast setup20%faster rate; no speed boost to haulers/processor/engineer.
PROPOSED NET ALTERNATIVE: Split-Haul Master,1active2passives. Own already-soaked catch lands in two portions during one normal retrieval. Deckhands can clear first while fisher still retrieves second; entire retrieval, carrying, uses and wear remain, basket block remains before next cycle. Team soak20%less and hauling20%faster same as Back-to-Back for fair signature comparison. Net alternative improves overlap but may be weaker at production pressure than Back-to-Back; recommend retaining Back-to-Back, do not replace without Mo.
PROPOSED CRAB SUPPORT: Cage Foreman,1team active2team passives. While leader assigned cages, all fishers gain optional assigned tending job on soaking cages; one tender per cage accelerates that cage soaking50%, but is actually occupied and unavailable for rods/net tasks during tending. Recovery remains required, can be performed by another available fisher/captain; new cage-support work/standing position and priorities need implementation and user selection. No spawning extra cages or unmanned recovery. Team passives across fishing only: rods bite wait and nets/cages soak10%less duration; rod reeling/net hauling/cage recovery10%faster rate. Excludes carrying/prep/repair speeds; additional operating cycles increase servicing demand. Distinct fullystaffed cage build supports multiple fishers rather than only fixed2operators, no automatic claiming already-approved new boat hardware.
NOT SELECTED: these are prototypes, rod/crab names/skills/passives and exactnumbers unapproved. Twin-hook timers/retained hook behavior need clear gameplay states; cage tending is proposed new job, not existing simultaneous rods/buoy fallback resold.
